### Durable facts
- **CHECK RECOMMENDED** — Transport choice | Do not rent a car for Athens — you won't need it and the traffic and parking are miserable. The metro from the airport is fast and cheap, and the historic center is walkable. If you continue to the islands, compare ferry times before you lock the next hotel, and book ahead in summer.

- **CHECK RECOMMENDED** — Day 1: Ancient Athens — the Acropolis at opening, the Acropolis Museum, the Ancient Agora, the old streets of Plaka and Anafiotika, and sunset from Filopappou Hill
  Source section: **The 2-Day Athens Itinerary at a Glance**. Original excerpt: “Day 1: Ancient Athens — the Acropolis at opening, the Acropolis Museum, the Ancient Agora, the old streets of Plaka and Anafiotika, and sunset from Filopappou Hill”
- **CHECK RECOMMENDED** — Climbing the Acropolis at midday. In summer it's hot, shadeless, and packed by late morning. Go at opening or skip to late afternoon — never the middle of the day.
  Source section: **What Most Visitors Get Wrong**. Original excerpt: “Climbing the Acropolis at midday. In summer it's hot, shadeless, and packed by late morning. Go at opening or skip to late afternoon — never the middle of the day.”

### Core promise

  Source section: **From Newark Liberty International (EWR)**. Original excerpt: “AirTrain + NJ Transit: $16–19 to Penn Station Manhattan. Journey time: 45–60 minutes. Good value if you are staying in Midtown or easily reach Penn Station.”

### Durable facts
- **CHECK RECOMMENDED** — Seasickness: if you're prone to it, take tablets 30–60 minutes before departure, choose a larger boat, and look at the horizon. The open Atlantic can be lively even on a "calm" day.
  Source section: **Practical Tips Before You Go**. Original excerpt: “Seasickness: if you're prone to it, take tablets 30–60 minutes before departure, choose a larger boat, and look at the horizon. The open Atlantic can be lively even on a ...”
- **CHECK RECOMMENDED** — Trip length | About 3 hours; morning departures tend to have calmer seas
  Source section: **Quick Summary**. Original excerpt: “Trip length | About 3 hours; morning departures tend to have calmer seas”
- **CHECK RECOMMENDED** — Book early and flexible: reserve a tour for early in your stay so you have backup days if the weather forces a reschedule.

### Useful information for the pin
- Start at Millennium Park. It's the most central stop and lets you walk through the park before boarding. The Bean in the morning before crowds is worth 15 minutes of your time even without the bus ticket.
- Treat it as a sampler, not an itinerary. Get on, stay on for the full loop without stopping (about 90 minutes), get a visual overview of the whole city, then get off at the one or two stops that look most interesting and spend real time there.
- Navy Pier. The Centennial Wheel, the food options, and the lakefront views east. Worth 60–90 minutes; more if you're eating.
- Museum Campus. The cluster of major museums on the lakefront south of the Loop — Field Museum, Shedd Aquarium, Adler Planetarium. Any one of these needs at least two hours; plan accordingly and don't try to do all three in a single afternoon.
- Before booking: compare total price with taxes, room type, cancellation terms, river-view upgrade cost, resort or destination fees, and recent guest comments for your exact dates.
